## Context

Wave 119 Ship 7 = `tools/cpa-cache-rate.py` (~85 LOC NEW). Closes operator's recurring "give me cache rate" question by aggregating CLIProxyAPI session-affinity cache hit/miss events per claude account from `Z:/claude-sota-installed/.cli-proxy-api/logs/main.log`.

Initial regex had a whitespace bug: `(cache hit|cache miss[^|]*)\|` expected NO space between `cache hit` and `|`, but actual log has ` cache hit | session=`. Smoke test caught it (11 miss / 0 hit when log has 1065 hits / 63 miss). Fixed by replacing `\|` with `\s*\|`. Re-smoke now shows correct rate (98.2% global, 567 hits / 11 miss in 720min window).

## 3 ADVERSARIAL AXES — find what's wrong

### AXIS 1 — Regex correctness + log-format edge cases

The fixed regex is:
```
^\[(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})\].*?session-affinity: (cache hit|cache miss[^|]*?)\s*\|.*?auth=(claude-[\w.@-]+\.json)
```

Find:
- Edge cases where the regex MISSES valid events (e.g., are there "cache hit, " or "cache hit;" variants?)
- Edge cases where the regex MIS-CLASSIFIES (e.g., does any line contain both "hit" and "miss" tokens?)
- Auth-name regex `claude-[\w.@-]+\.json` — does `\w` include `.`? Are the dot+@+dash chars sufficient for ALL claude account file names (`claude-mr.euphoriaincarnate@gmail.com.json` / `claude-aesthetic9c@gmail.com.json` / `claude-739955940fc@gmail.com.json`)?
- Does the timestamp parse handle 02:00 daylight-saving anomalies? (`datetime.strptime` is naive)
- The `kind = "hit" if "hit" in m.group(2) else "miss"` classifier — does any "miss" capture group contain "hit" substring? (e.g. could `cache miss, new binding` ever be `cache miss, hit-rate-degraded`?)

### AXIS 2 — Smoke-empty-window + edge-case behavior

The smoke test with `--since-mins 240` returned 0 events (cache events were 7-8h old per pre-equalization sticky session). The wider `--since-mins 720` window returned 605 hits / 11 miss / 98.2% rate.

Find:
- Should the script error or warn when 0 events found? (current behavior: prints "(no session-affinity events in window)" and returns 0)
- Is `tail last N lines` strategy correct for log files that may rotate? `--lines 5000` default is small; CPA log can grow fast under heavy traffic.
- For 1065 cache hit lines in a 8-day log, default `--since-mins 60` will likely find 0 events (last activity was 7-8h ago). Default may be misleading. Should default be `--since-mins 1440` (24h)?
- The `readlines()[-args.lines:]` is O(file_size) memory load. For a 100MB log, this hangs. Should it stream from end?

### AXIS 3 — Cite discipline + commit-readiness

- The cite line `# Cite: CLIProxyAPI sdk/cliproxy/auth/selector.go:498-535 @ HEAD 785b00c3` — verify line range matches actual selector.go (the script docstring claims `selector.go:498-535`; the log lines themselves cite `selector.go:503` for hits and `selector.go:535` for miss — does 498-535 actually contain those emit sites?)
- HEAD SHA `785b00c3` — is that the current pin or stale? This script ships under cardinal-rule-1 cite-trail discipline.
- For commit body: this is a NEW tool file (~85 LOC). Per `codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md §Mechanical-mirror exception`, predicate (1) ≤24 LOC delta FAILS (85 LOC > 24). So Pattern A NEEDS-REVISION fix-forward path applies if you find any prescription. If no prescription and you APPROVE, commit lands as-is.

   1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2: """cpa-cache-rate — aggregate session-affinity cache hit rate per claude account.
   3: 
   4: Wave 119 Ship 7. Closes operator's recurring "give me cache rate" question by
   5: parsing CLIProxyAPI session-affinity log lines from main.log.
   6: 
   7: Cite: CLIProxyAPI `sdk/cliproxy/auth/selector.go:498-535 @ HEAD 785b00c3` —
   8: session-affinity emits "session-affinity: cache hit" / "cache miss, new
   9: binding" lines per request.
  10: 
  11: Usage: python tools/cpa-cache-rate.py [--lines N] [--since-mins M]
  12:   --lines N      tail last N log lines (default: 5000)
  13:   --since-mins M only count requests from last M minutes (default: 60)
  14: 
  15: Output: per-account total/hits/misses + global hit rate.
  16: """
  17: from __future__ import annotations
  18: import re
  19: import sys
  20: import argparse
  21: from collections import defaultdict
  22: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  23: from pathlib import Path
  24: 
  25: LOG = Path("Z:/claude-sota-installed/.cli-proxy-api/logs/main.log")
  26: HIT_RE = re.compile(
  27:     r"^\[(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})\].*?session-affinity: (cache hit|cache miss[^|]*?)\s*\|.*?auth=(claude-[\w.@-]+\.json)"
  28: )
  29: 
  30: 
  31: def main() -> int:
  32:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="cpa-cache-rate — aggregate session-affinity cache hit rate per claude account")
  33:     ap.add_argument("--lines", type=int, default=5000, help="tail last N lines")
  34:     ap.add_argument("--since-mins", type=int, default=60, help="window in minutes")
  35:     args = ap.parse_args()
  36: 
  37:     if not LOG.exists():
  38:         print(f"ERR: log not found at {LOG}", file=sys.stderr)
  39:         return 1
  40: 
  41:     cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=args.since_mins)
  42:     by_acct: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(lambda: {"hit": 0, "miss": 0})
  43: 
  44:     with LOG.open("r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fh:
  45:         tail = fh.readlines()[-args.lines :]
  46: 
  47:     for line in tail:
  48:         m = HIT_RE.match(line)
  49:         if not m:
  50:             continue
  51:         try:
  52:             ts = datetime.strptime(m.group(1), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
  53:         except ValueError:
  54:             continue
  55:         if ts < cutoff:
  56:             continue
  57:         kind = "hit" if "hit" in m.group(2) else "miss"
  58:         by_acct[m.group(3)][kind] += 1
  59: 
  60:     total_hits = sum(v["hit"] for v in by_acct.values())
  61:     total_miss = sum(v["miss"] for v in by_acct.values())
  62:     total = total_hits + total_miss
  63: 
  64:     print(f"=== CPA cache-rate (last {args.since_mins}min, {args.lines} log lines tailed) ===")
  65:     if total == 0:
  66:         print("(no session-affinity events in window)")
  67:         return 0
  68: 
  69:     print(f"{'auth':<55} {'hits':>6} {'miss':>5} {'rate':>7}")
  70:     print("-" * 78)
  71:     for acct in sorted(by_acct):
  72:         h, m = by_acct[acct]["hit"], by_acct[acct]["miss"]
  73:         n = h + m
  74:         rate = f"{h / n * 100:5.1f}%" if n else "  n/a"
  75:         print(f"{acct:<55} {h:>6} {m:>5} {rate:>7}")
  76:     print("-" * 78)
  77:     rate_pct = total_hits / total * 100 if total else 0
  78:     print(f"{'TOTAL':<55} {total_hits:>6} {total_miss:>5} {rate_pct:5.1f}%")
  79:     return 0
  80: 
  81: 
  82: if __name__ == "__main__":
  83:     sys.exit(main())

"C:\\Program Files\\PowerShell\\7\\pwsh.exe" -Command "@'
import re
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('.cli-proxy-api/logs/main.log')
rx=re.compile(r'"'^'"\\[(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2} \\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2})\\].*?session-affinity: (cache hit|cache miss["'^|]*?)'"\\s*\\|.*?auth=(claude-[\\w.@-]+\\.json)')
all_cache=0
all_claude=0
matched=0
unmatched=[]
auths=set()
for i,line in enumerate(p.open('r',encoding='utf-8',errors='replace'),1):
    if 'session-affinity: cache' not in line:
        continue
    all_cache+=1
    if 'auth=claude-' in line:
        all_claude+=1
        m=re.search(r'auth=(["'^ ]+)'"', line)
        if m: auths.add(m.group(1))
        if rx.match(line):
            matched+=1
        else:
            unmatched.append((i,line.strip()))
print('all_cache', all_cache)
print('all_claude', all_claude)
print('matched_by_script_regex', matched)
print('unmatched_claude', len(unmatched))
print('auths', ','.join(sorted(auths)))
for i,line in unmatched[:20]: print(f'{i}:{line}')

 532: 		return nil, err
 533: 	}
 534: 	s.cache.Set(cacheKey, auth.ID)
 535: 	entry.Infof("session-affinity: cache miss, new binding | session=%s auth=%s provider=%s model=%s", truncateSessionID(primaryID), auth.ID, provider, model)

**Classifying cache hits and misses**

I'm thinking about how to classify hit/miss events in caching scenarios. If something is a "cache hit but," it might need a separate classification. The script counts reselected items as misses, which probably means it's rating things lower than they should be. If we're treating something as a hit, we need explicit classification for it. It seems "auth unavailable, reselected" is a tricky case since it's a hit but not really a valid one. I should clarify this further!
**Clarifying cache classifications**

I'm considering how to classify cache events correctly. If I label something as a hit just because it contains "hit," that could be misleading since cached auth wasn't actually used. It's probably better to match exact action labels for classification: I might call `cache hit |` a hit, while `cache miss, new binding |` and `cache hit but auth unavailable, reselected |` could be misses. I should also think about whether to include a separate column for reselected items. Plus, I need to check if there's a fallback cache hit in the logs!

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime,timedelta
import re
now=datetime.now(); cutoff=now-timedelta(minutes=720)
rx_ts=re.compile(r'"'^'"\\[(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2} \\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2})\\]')
cache=[]
for i,line in enumerate(Path('.cli-proxy-api/logs/main.log').open(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace'),1):

    ts=datetime.strptime(rx_ts.match(line).group(1),'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

from datetime import datetime,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter
import re
cutoff=datetime.now()-timedelta(minutes=720)
msg=Counter(); script=Counter()
rx_script=re.compile(r'"'^'"\\[(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2} \\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2})\\].*?session-affinity: (cache hit|cache miss["'^|]*?)'"\\s*\\|.*?auth=(claude-[\\w.@-]+\\.json)')
for line in Path('.cli-proxy-api/logs/main.log').open(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace'):
    if 'session-affinity: ' not in line or 'auth=claude-' not in line: continue
    ts=datetime.strptime(line[1:20], '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
    if ts<cutoff: continue
    m=re.search(r'session-affinity: (.*?) \\|', line)
    if m: msg[m.group(1)] += 1
    sm=rx_script.match(line)
    if sm: script['hit' if 'hit' in sm.group(2) else 'miss']+=1
print('cutoff', cutoff.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'))
print('messages', dict(msg))
print('script', dict(script))
